Persona 2: Innocent Sin

100% finite-content completion on the chosen PSP route, spoiler-free · 22 areas · 1480 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead — it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Open the spoiler-free interactive guide

PSP Route and Save Safety

You are here when: The title screen, difficulty menu, save list, and PPSSPP memory-card folder before starting.

The PSP release is the definitive route selected in games.json.

Use the 2011 PSP Release

Platform Route

HintThe chosen route uses the enhanced portable edition.
WherePlay the PSP release on hardware or PPSSPP with one clean memory-card set.
Full detailKeep the PSP game unmodified apart from display scaling, record the region and build, and archive the complete save directory before changing emulator versions. Increased internal resolution is allowed; gameplay cheats are not part of the completion record.

Complete a Hard-Difficulty File

Difficulty Record

HintThe highest built-in setting provides the route's full combat test.
WhereBegin or switch a protected PSP branch to Hard and finish that file.
Full detailUse the same finite-content route plan on Hard, preserve the cleared system data, and record the difficulty screen with the final save. No additional reward is assumed; this row documents the available challenge mode itself.

Record the Original PlayStation Boundary

Edition Boundary

HintThe earlier Japanese release is not the selected completion route.
WhereTreat the 1999 PlayStation version as historical comparison only.
Full detailDo not merge original-release balance, interface, or localization differences into the PSP checklist. Start a separate archival file only if you intentionally decide to compare editions later.

Create a Rotating PSP Save Archive (missable)

Save Safety

HintDialogue, timed areas, rumors, and rare mutations create one-way completion gates.
WhereKeep branches before each personal-choice scene, timed dungeon exit, rumor spread, and late temple sequence.
Full detailMaintain at least eight named save branches plus an external copy of the PPSSPP memory-card directory. Label copies by physical place, and never overwrite the only file before a rumor, gallery, Persona, or weapon result is confirmed.

Personal Choice and Gallery Gates

You are here when: The secret lounge, outdoor concert stage, museum catwalk anteroom, mountain path, and Gallery menu.

Branch the museum choice because its two movies conflict on a single pass through the scene.

Rhadamanthus Prime (missable)

Persona Gate

HintLet the affected companion handle the immediate problem without intervention.
WhereAt Club Zodiac secret lounge, choose “Wait and see what happens.”
Full detailSave before the scene at Club Zodiac secret lounge, select “Wait and see what happens,” and keep the resulting flag through the later reward check. Confirm Rhadamanthus Prime becomes available before deleting the pre-choice branch.

Bonds of Flame: Tatsuya (missable)

Gallery Entry

HintOne version of the museum choice unlocks a character-specific movie.
WhereAt Aerospace Museum catwalk anteroom, choose “I'll jump down” on a branch save.
Full detailSave before the catwalk choice, select “I'll jump down,” finish the scene, and verify Bonds of Flame: Tatsuya appears in the Gallery. Restore the backup afterward so the prime-Persona answer can also be preserved.

Bonds of Flame: Maya (missable)

Gallery Entry

HintThe companion-led version protects both a movie and a Persona flag.
WhereAt Aerospace Museum catwalk anteroom, choose “Leave this to Maya” on the main branch.
Full detailRestore the catwalk backup, select “Leave this to Maya,” complete the scene, and verify Bonds of Flame: Maya appears in the Gallery. Continue this branch for the associated prime-Persona flag.

Mt. Iwato and Temple Upgrades

You are here when: Mt. Iwato's B1F side passages and Reflecting Pool, followed by the elemental temple interiors and Velvet Room.

The four masks, one equipped-Persona battle, and three temple pairings cover every ultimate form.

Pink Azaz Mask (missable)

Key Mask

HintOne of Mt. Iwato's side rooms contains a colored mask needed later.
WhereSearch Mt. Iwato's secret rooms for Pink Azaz Mask before entering the Reflecting Pool.
Full detailExplore every B1F branch, enter the concealed room on that route, and take Pink Azaz Mask from its item box. Confirm it remains in the key-item inventory; leaving for the Reflecting Pool without it permanently closes one character's ultimate upgrade on that file.

Apollo (missable)

Ultimate Persona

HintA complete four-mask set supplies this late character upgrade.
WhereCarry all four colored masks through the Mt. Iwato reward check to receive Apollo.
Full detailDo not enter the Reflecting Pool until Pink Azaz, Yellow Owl, Red Eagle, and Blue Swan are all present. Complete the area, verify Apollo is granted, and make a protected save before returning or mutating any Persona.

Chronos (missable)

Ultimate Persona

HintOne character's final upgrade uses mutation rather than the four-mask grant.
WhereEquip Hermes for the key Aquarius Temple battle, then visit the Velvet Room.
Full detailBefore the Aquarius Temple's decisive encounter, put Hermes on Jun and finish the battle with it equipped so its mutation becomes available. Save, enter the Velvet Room, select Mutate Persona, change Hermes into Chronos, and confirm the result before overwriting.

Hades Enhanced Form (missable)

Persona Upgrade

HintKeeping this ultimate Persona equipped in its matching temple adds a final improvement.
WhereEquip Hades for the key encounter in Scorpio Temple.
Full detailEnter Scorpio Temple with Hades assigned to its intended character, keep it equipped through the area's decisive battle, and confirm the improved form or ability afterward. Restore the pre-temple save if the upgrade does not register.

Velvet Room Mutation Forms

You are here when: Ordinary encounter floors, battle result screens, and the Velvet Room's Mutate Persona menu.

Twelve minor-arcana forms require random mutation branches.

Budai (missable)

Mutation Persona

HintThis minor-arcana Persona appears through a random mutation rather than normal summoning.
WhereUse Pixie in a battle-ending Fusion Spell until its mutation offers Budai.
Full detailRaise Pixie, save outside battle, and repeatedly finish encounters with a Fusion Spell involving it. When a mutation triggers, visit the Velvet Room and accept only the Budai result; reload if the random branch produces a different form.

Climax Theater

You are here when: The city-map theater lobby, ticket menu, membership display, and the three localized quest interiors.

The localized PSP route has three built-in quests and no in-quest saving.

School of the Heart (Part 1) (missable)

Theater Quest

HintThe PSP-exclusive theater contains a finite side scenario behind a paid ticket.
WhereBuy School of the Heart (Part 1) for 10,000 yen at the Climax Theater.
Full detailPrepare and save in the main game before buying the 10,000-yen ticket, because saving is disabled inside the quest. Finish School of the Heart (Part 1) without abandoning it, confirm membership points are awarded, and return to the theater menu.

School of the Heart (Part 2) (missable)

Theater Quest

HintThe PSP-exclusive theater contains a finite side scenario behind a paid ticket.
WhereBuy School of the Heart (Part 2) for 10,000 yen at the Climax Theater.
Full detailPrepare and save in the main game before buying the 10,000-yen ticket, because saving is disabled inside the quest. Finish School of the Heart (Part 2) without abandoning it, confirm membership points are awarded, and return to the theater menu.

Persona Thief (missable)

Theater Quest

HintThe PSP-exclusive theater contains a finite side scenario behind a paid ticket.
WhereBuy Persona Thief for 10,000 yen at the Climax Theater.
Full detailPrepare and save in the main game before buying the 10,000-yen ticket, because saving is disabled inside the quest. Finish Persona Thief without abandoning it, confirm membership points are awarded, and return to the theater menu.

Maximum Theater Rank Dialogue

Theater Rank

HintRepeated clears increase membership rank even after the three unique quests are done.
WhereReplay any completed theater quest until the receptionist's final rank dialogue appears.
Full detailAfter clearing all three unique quests, repeat the fastest safe quest and never use Abandon. Check the receptionist after each points award; stop when her dialogue no longer advances, since rank changes conversation but does not unlock another localized quest.

Japanese Theater DLC Boundary

Regional Boundary

HintThe Japanese edition had downloadable and custom-quest functions absent from localized releases.
WhereDo not present unavailable regional files as routine PSP completion.
Full detailNEEDS_VERIFICATION

Legendary Weapon Rumor Archive

You are here when: The Abandoned Factory, city weapon shops, shrine path, park bulletin board, rumormongers, and Kuzunoha Detective Agency.

Branch each weapon's rumor so all six finite forms are documented without sacrificing the strongest main-save version.

Legendary Katana (missable)

Legendary Weapon

HintA late rumor chain and an optional exploration ledger reveal this character weapon.
WhereOwner: Tatsuya; gate notes: factory notes 1-11; hidden switch rumor.
Full detailKeep a pre-rumor save, satisfy factory notes 1-11; hidden switch rumor, spread the resulting weapon rumor through Chief Todoroki, and claim or buy Legendary Katana. Equip it on Tatsuya and record the base form before changing its rumor property.

Legendary Katana: Inferior Form (missable)

Weapon Form

HintDemon information can rewrite this weapon's element or quality.
WhereObtain and spread the inferior weapon rumor while Legendary Katana is owned.
Full detailSave with Legendary Katana equipped, contact eligible late-game demons until the inferior version is reported, and spread that version. Open the equipment screen to record the changed name and statistics, then restore the pre-rumor branch for the next form.

Demon Analysis Registry

You are here when: Random encounters, Contact responses, battle results, and the Analyze menu throughout every dungeon.

A full entry requires contact, observed skills, item evidence, and one defeat.

Demon Analysis

HintUse fighting and Contact to complete this demon's Analyze record.
WhereAnalyze target: level 49 01.0 Magician demon.
Full detailEncounter Manannan, perform Contact once for personality data, allow each visible skill to be used, obtain its item through a drop or pact reward, and defeat it once for statistics. Reopen Analyze and confirm the record is complete before leaving its final available dungeon.

Fusion Spell Registry

You are here when: Party command order, Persona skill lists, battle animations, and the recorded Fusion Spell menu.

Perform every finite PSP combination once so it becomes available from the menu.

Blazing Burst

Fusion Spell

HintArrange compatible Persona skills in the correct turn order to reveal this combination.
WhereRequired sequence length: 2 action(s).
Full detailSet slot 1 to Any fire spell. Set slot 2 to Any fire spell. Execute the ordered actions in one turn, let Blazing Burst activate, then confirm it appears in the Fusion Spell list before ending the session.
